ARK_recalc_ratio = {
	# instead, it will compare ARK_Attacker_Strength with ARK_Defender_Strength, but with usual variables instead of temporary ones
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				ARK_set_target = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					global.ARK_Dock_Control = 1
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					ROOT = {
						tag = UFA
					}
				}
				set_variable = {
					ARK_Ratio = 100
				}
			}
			else = {
				# calculate ratio
				set_temp_variable = {
					ARK_Attacker_Strength = ARK_Free_Units
				}
				set_temp_variable = {
					ARK_Defender_Strength = UFA.ARK_Free_Units
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							UFA.ARK_Free_Units = 0
						}
					}
					set_variable = {
						ARK_Ratio = 100
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = {
					ARK_Attacker_Bonuses = 1
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_operative_leader = {
							operative_leader_operation = operation_contest_for_ARK_1
							has_trait = operative_demolition_expert
							has_trait = operative_ark_specialist
						}
					}
					add_to_temp_variable = {
						ARK_Attacker_Bonuses = 0.15
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_operative_leader = {
							operative_leader_operation = operation_contest_for_ARK_1
							has_trait = operative_infiltrator
							has_trait = operative_ark_specialist
						}
					}
					add_to_temp_variable = {
						ARK_Attacker_Bonuses = 0.15
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_operative_leader = {
							operative_leader_operation = operation_contest_for_ARK_1
							has_trait = operative_tough
							has_trait = operative_ark_specialist
						}
					}
					add_to_temp_variable = {
						ARK_Attacker_Bonuses = 0.1
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_operative_leader = {
							operative_leader_operation = operation_contest_for_ARK_1
							has_trait = operative_commando
							has_trait = operative_ark_specialist
						}
					}
					add_to_temp_variable = {
						ARK_Attacker_Bonuses = 0.25
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_operative_leader = {
							operative_leader_operation = operation_contest_for_ARK_1
							has_trait = operative_fear_of_pain
							has_trait = operative_ark_specialist
						}
					}
					add_to_temp_variable = {
						ARK_Attacker_Bonuses = -0.2
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_operative_leader = {
							operative_leader_operation = operation_contest_for_ARK_1
							has_trait = operative_natural_orator
							has_trait = operative_ark_specialist
						}
					}
					add_to_temp_variable = {
						ARK_Attacker_Bonuses = 0.1
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_done_agency_upgrade = satellite_network
					}
					add_to_temp_variable = {
						ARK_Attacker_Bonuses = 0.125
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_done_agency_upgrade = nanite_disguise
					}
					add_to_temp_variable = {
						ARK_Attacker_Bonuses = 0.125
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_done_agency_upgrade = cyberspace
					}
					add_to_temp_variable = {
						ARK_Attacker_Bonuses = 0.1
					}
				}
				if = {

									has_global_flag = Tails_on_ARK
								}
							}
						}
					}
					add_to_temp_variable = {
						ARK_Defender_Bonuses = 0.25
					}
					add_to_temp_variable = {
						ARK_Defender_Strength = 10
					}
				}
				multiply_temp_variable = {
					ARK_Attacker_Strength = ARK_Attacker_Bonuses
				}
				multiply_temp_variable = {
					ARK_Defender_Strength = ARK_Defender_Bonuses
				}
				divide_temp_variable = {
					ARK_Attacker_Strength = ARK_Defender_Strength
				}
				set_variable = {
					ARK_Ratio = ARK_Attacker_Strength
				}
				multiply_variable = {
					ARK_Ratio = 50
				}
				clamp_variable = {
					var = ARK_Ratio
					min = 0
					max = 100
				}
			}
		}
	}

ARK_the_end_of_the_world = {
	every_country = {
		country_event = ark_doomsday.1
		delete_unit = {
		}
		destroy_ships = {
			type = destroyer
			count = all
		}
		destroy_ships = {
			type = light_cruiser
			count = all
		}
		destroy_ships = {
			type = heavy_cruiser
			count = all
		}
		destroy_ships = {
			type = battle_cruiser
			count = all
		}
		destroy_ships = {
			type = battleship
			count = all
		}
		destroy_ships = {
			type = carrier
			count = all
		}
		destroy_ships = {
			type = submarine
			count = all
		}
		destroy_ships = {
			type = ship_hull_submarine
			count = all
		}
		destroy_ships = {
			type = ship_hull_light
			count = all
		}
		destroy_ships = {
			type = ship_hull_heavy
			count = all
		}
		destroy_ships = {
			type = ship_hull_carrier
			count = all
		}
		destroy_ships = {
			type = convoy
			count = all
		}
		set_political_power = -500
		set_stability = 0
		set_war_support = 0
		set_fuel = 0
		add_manpower = -99999999
		every_state = {
			add_manpower = -99999999
			cancel_resistance = yes
			add_dynamic_modifier = {
				modifier = Blasted_by_ARK
			}
		}
		every_character = {
			# limit = {
			# 	NOT = {
			# 	}
			# }
			retire = yes
		}
	}
	ROOT = {
		every_state = {
			PREV = {
				launch_nuke = {
					state = PREV
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

ARK_recalc_reinforcements_training_slots = {
	set_variable = {
		ARK_Reinforcements_Training_Slots = num_operative_slots
	}
	set_variable = {
		ARK_additional_slots = agency_upgrade_number
	}
	divide_variable = {
		ARK_additional_slots = 4
	}
	add_to_variable = {
		ARK_Reinforcements_Training_Slots = ARK_additional_slots
	}
	round_variable = ARK_Reinforcements_Training_Slots
	clamp_variable = {
		var = ARK_Reinforcements_Training_Slots
		min = 0
		max = 25
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_Training_Slots < 1
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			ARK_Reinforcements_Training_Slots = 1
		}
	}
}

ARK_process_reinforcement_queue = {
	# Проверяем, есть ли кого ставить и есть ли куда ставить
	# Поочередно проверяем миссии от 1 до 25 и запускаем на первом свободном слоте
	# Слот 1
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue > 0
			}
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_Training_Slots > ARK_Occupied_Reinforcements_Training_Slots
			}
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_1_decision_is_active
			}
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue = 1
		}
		activate_decision = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_1
	}
	# Слот 2
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue > 0
			}
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_Training_Slots > ARK_Occupied_Reinforcements_Training_Slots
			}
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_2_decision_is_active
			}
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue = 1
		}
		activate_decision = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_2
	}
	# Слот 3
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue > 0
			}
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_Training_Slots > ARK_Occupied_Reinforcements_Training_Slots
			}
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_3_decision_is_active
			}
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue = 1
		}
		activate_decision = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_3
	}
	# Слот 4
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue > 0
			}
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_Training_Slots > ARK_Occupied_Reinforcements_Training_Slots
			}
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_4_decision_is_active
			}
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue = 1
		}
		activate_decision = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_4
	}
	# Слот 5
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue > 0
			}
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_Training_Slots > ARK_Occupied_Reinforcements_Training_Slots
			}
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_5_decision_is_active
			}
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue = 1
		}
		activate_decision = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_5
	}
	# Слот 6
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue > 0
			}
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_Training_Slots > ARK_Occupied_Reinforcements_Training_Slots
			}
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_6_decision_is_active
			}
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue = 1
		}
		activate_decision = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_6
	}
	# Слот 7
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue > 0
			}
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_Training_Slots > ARK_Occupied_Reinforcements_Training_Slots
			}
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_7_decision_is_active
			}
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue = 1
		}
		activate_decision = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_7
	}
	# Слот 8
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue > 0
			}
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_Training_Slots > ARK_Occupied_Reinforcements_Training_Slots
			}
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_8_decision_is_active
			}
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue = 1
		}
		activate_decision = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_8
	}
	# Слот 9
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue > 0
			}
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_Training_Slots > ARK_Occupied_Reinforcements_Training_Slots
			}
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_9_decision_is_active
			}
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue = 1
		}
		activate_decision = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_9
	}
	# Слот 10
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue > 0
			}
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_Training_Slots > ARK_Occupied_Reinforcements_Training_Slots
			}
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_10_decision_is_active
			}
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue = 1
		}
		activate_decision = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_10
	}
	# Слот 11
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue > 0
			}
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_Training_Slots > ARK_Occupied_Reinforcements_Training_Slots
			}
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_11_decision_is_active
			}
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue = 1
		}
		activate_decision = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_11
	}
	# Слот 12
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue > 0
			}
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_Training_Slots > ARK_Occupied_Reinforcements_Training_Slots
			}
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_12_decision_is_active
			}
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue = 1
		}
		activate_decision = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_12
	}
	# Слот 13
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue > 0
			}
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_Training_Slots > ARK_Occupied_Reinforcements_Training_Slots
			}
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_13_decision_is_active
			}
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue = 1
		}
		activate_decision = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_13
	}
	# Слот 14
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue > 0
			}
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_Training_Slots > ARK_Occupied_Reinforcements_Training_Slots
			}
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_14_decision_is_active
			}
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue = 1
		}
		activate_decision = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_14
	}
	# Слот 15
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue > 0
			}
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_Training_Slots > ARK_Occupied_Reinforcements_Training_Slots
			}
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_15_decision_is_active
			}
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue = 1
		}
		activate_decision = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_15
	}
	# Слот 16
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue > 0
			}
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_Training_Slots > ARK_Occupied_Reinforcements_Training_Slots
			}
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_16_decision_is_active
			}
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue = 1
		}
		activate_decision = ARK_prepare_the_reinforcements_16
	}
	# Слот 17
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_In_Queue > 0
			}
			check_variable = {
				ARK_Reinforcements_Training_Slots > ARK_Occupied_Reinforcements_Training_Slots
			}
			NOT = {
